Look for patterns like 'Txn Ref: ABC123' or 'Transaction No ABC123' etc.\n    lines = text.splitlines()\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n        low = line.lower()\n        if any(label in low for label in ['txn ref', 'transaction no', 'ref no']):\n            # try same line after colon or after label\n            m = re.search(r'[:\\-]\\s*([A-Z0-9]+)', line, re.IGNORECASE)\n            if m:\n                return m.group(1).strip()\n            # try after space\n            parts = line.split()\n            if len(parts) >= 2:\n                candidate = parts[-1]\n                if re.fullmatch(r'[A-Z0-9]+', candidate):\n                    return candidate.strip()\n            # try next line\n            if i + 1 < len(lines):\n                nxt = lines[i+1].strip()\n                if re.fullmatch(r'[A-Z0-9]+', nxt):\n                    return nxt\n    return ''\n\ndef parse_date_string(ds):\n    # Attempt formats in order, returning ISO string or empty.\n    for fmt in ['%d/%m/%Y', '%d-%m-%Y', '%m/%d/%Y', '%Y-%m-%d']:\n        try:\n            dt = datetime.strptime(ds, fmt)\n            # when ambiguous (both day and month <=12) we already prioritize DD/MM/Y above MM/DD/Y.\n            return dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    return ''\n\ndef extract_date(text):\n    lines = text.splitlines()\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n        low = line.lower()\n        if 'sale date' in low or 'date' == low.strip() or low.startswith('date'):\n            # try to find a date token on the same line\n            m = re.search(r'(\\d{2}[/-]\\d{2}[/-]\\d{4}|\\d{4}[/-]\\d{2}[/-]\\d{2})', line)\n            if m:\n                iso = parse_date_string(m.group(1))\n                if iso:\n                    return iso\n            # else look at next line\n            if i + 1 < len(lines):\n                nxt = lines[i+1]\n                m = re.search(r'(\\d{2}[/-]\\d{2}[/-]\\d{4}|\\d{4}[/-]\\d{2}[/-]\\d{2})', nxt)\n                if m:\n                    iso = parse_date_string(m.group(1))\n                    if iso:\n                        return iso\n    return ''\n\ndef extract_total_amount(text):\n    # Prepare lines without discount/tax etc.\n    lines = text.splitlines()\n    candidates = []\n    for idx, line in enumerate(lines):\n        low = line.lower()\n        # skip if any ignore keyword present\n        if any(kw in low for kw in ['discount', 'cashback', 'savings', 'loyalty', 'tax']):\n            continue\n        # check for keywords\n        kw_priority = None\n        if 'grand total' in low:\n            kw_priority = 1\n        elif 'total amount' in low:\n            kw_priority = 2\n        elif 'amount paid' in low:\n            kw_priority = 3\n        elif re.search(r'\\btotal\\b', low):\n            kw_priority = 4\n        if kw_priority is not None:\n            # try to find number on same line\n            m = re.search(r'([\\d,]+\\.\\d{2})', line)\n            if m:\n                candidates.append((kw_priority, m.group(1)))\n                continue\n            # else next line\n            if idx + 1 < len(lines):\n                nxt = lines[idx+1]\n                m = re.search(r'([\\d,]+\\.\\d{2})', nxt)\n                if m:\n                    candidates.append((kw_priority, m.group(1)))\n    if not candidates:\n        return ''\n    # pick lowest priority number (most specific)\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    raw = candidates[0][1]\n    # clean commas and $ sign\n    clean = raw.replace(',', '').replace('$', '')\n    try:\n        val = float(clean)\n        return f\"{val:.2f}\"\n    except Exception:\n        return ''\n\ndef process_images(images):\n    rows = []\n    for img in images:\n        txt = ocr_image(img['abs_path'])\n        if not txt:\n            continue\n        if not is_fuel_receipt(txt):\n            print(f\"Skipping non-receipt: {img['relative_path']}\")\n            continue\n        txn = extract_txn_ref(txt)\n        date = extract_date(txt)\n        total = extract_total_amount(txt)\n        rows.append({\n            'batch_name': img['batch_name'],\n            'relative_path': img['relative_path'],\n            'txn_ref': txn,\n            'date': date,\n            'total_amount': total,\n        })\n        print(f\"Processed {img['relative_path']}: txn={txn} date={date} total={total}\")\n    return rows\n\ndef deduplicate(rows):\n    seen = set()\n    out = []\n    for row in rows:\n        ref = row['txn_ref']\n        if ref and ref in seen:\n            continue\n        if ref:\n            seen.add(ref)\n        out.append(row)\n    return out\n\ndef write_excel(rows):\n    df = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=['batch_name', 'relative_path', 'txn_ref', 'date', 'total_amount'])\n    df.sort_values('relative_path', inplace=True)\n    #
